FILE: .github\workflows\deploy.yml
==========================================
name: Deploy to GitHub Pages

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read
  pages: write
  id-token: write

concurrency:
  group: pages
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  build:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Setup Node
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: 20
          cache: npm

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: npm ci

      - name: Build
        run: npm run build

      - name: Upload artifact
        uses: actions/upload-pages-artifact@v3
        with:
          path: ./dist

  deploy:
    needs: build
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    environment:
      name: github-pages
      url: ${{ steps.deployment.outputs.page_url }}
    steps:
      - name: Deploy to GitHub Pages
        id: deployment
        uses: actions/deploy-pages@v4

import { Image } from 'astro:assets';
import Container from '@/components/Container.astro';
import { categories } from '@/content/categories';
import { withBase } from '@/utils/seo';

const images = import.meta.glob<{ default: ImageMetadata }>(
  '@/assets/images/cat-*.jpg',
  { eager: true }
);

function getCategoryImage(name: string) {
  const key = Object.keys(images).find((k) => k.includes(`/${name}.`));
  if (!key) {
    throw new Error(
      `РќРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅРѕ С„РѕС‚Рѕ РєР°С‚РµРіРѕСЂРёРё: ${name}. РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЊ, С‡С‚Рѕ С„Р°Р№Р» src/assets/images/${name}.jpg СЃСѓС‰РµСЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚.`
    );
  }
  return images[key].default;
}
---

<section class="categories">
  <Container>
    <header class="categories__header">
      <h2 class="categories__title">РџРѕРїСѓР»СЏСЂРЅС‹Рµ РєР°С‚РµРіРѕСЂРёРё</h2>
      <a href={withBase('/catalog')} class="categories__all">
        РЎРјРѕС‚СЂРµС‚СЊ РІСЃРµ
      </a>
    </header>

    <ul class="categories__list">
      {categories.map((cat) => (
        <li>
          <a href={withBase('/catalog')} class="categories__item">
            <span class="categories__media">
              <Image
                src={getCategoryImage(cat.image)}
                alt={cat.title}
                widths={[240, 360, 520, 720]}
                sizes="(max-width: 639px) 50vw, (max-width: 1023px) 33vw, 25vw"
                format="webp"
                quality={82}
                loading="lazy"
                class="categories__image"
              />
            </span>
            <span class="categories__name">{cat.title}</span>
          </a>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  </Container>
</section>

<header class="site-header" data-header>
  <Container>
    <div class="site-header__inner">
      <a
        href={withBase('/')}
        class="site-header__logo"
        aria-label={`${site.name} вЂ” РЅР° РіР»Р°РІРЅСѓСЋ`}
      >

        aria-controls="primary-navigation"
        data-burger
      >
        <span></span>
        <span></span>
        <span></span>
      </button>

      <nav
        id="primary-navigation"
        class="site-header__nav"

        data-nav
      >
        <ul class="site-header__nav-list">
          {navigation.map((item) => (
            <li>
              <a
                href={withBase(item.href)}
                class:list={[
                  'site-header__nav-link',
                  { 'is-active': currentPath === withBase(item.href).replace(/\/$/, '') },
                ]}
              >
                {item.label}
                {item.note && <span class="site-header__nav-note">{item.note}</span>}
              </a>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>

        <a href={withBase('/sell')} class="site-header__cta">РЎРґР°С‚СЊ С‚РѕРІР°СЂ</a>
      </nav>
    </div>
  </Container>
</header>

<script>
  // -------- РЎРєСЂРѕР»Р»-СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ С€Р°РїРєРё --------
  const header = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-header]');
  if (header) {
    const onScroll = () => {
      if (window.scrollY > 20) header.classList.add('is-scrolled');
      else header.classList.remove('is-scrolled');
    };
    onScroll();
    window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true });
  }

  // -------- РњРѕР±РёР»СЊРЅРѕРµ РјРµРЅСЋ --------
  const burger = document.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-burger]');
  const nav = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-nav]');

  if (burger && nav) {
    const toggle = (open: boolean) => {
      burger.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(open));
      burger.setAttribute('aria-label', open ? 'Р—Р°РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РјРµРЅСЋ' : 'РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РјРµРЅСЋ');
      nav.classList.toggle('is-open', open);
      document.body.style.overflow = open ? 'hidden' : '';
    };

    burger.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const open = burger.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true';
      toggle(!open);
    });

    nav.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if ((e.target as HTMLElement).closest('a')) toggle(false);
    });

    window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') toggle(false);
    });
  }
</script>

import { Image } from 'astro:assets';
import Container from '@/components/Container.astro';
import { showcase } from '@/content/showcase';
import { withBase } from '@/utils/seo';

// РЎРѕР±РёСЂР°РµРј РІСЃРµ С„РѕС‚Рѕ С‚РѕРІР°СЂРѕРІ вЂ” РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј С‚Рµ Р¶Рµ cat-*.jpg, С‡С‚Рѕ Рё РґР»СЏ РєР°С‚РµРіРѕСЂРёР№
const images = import.meta.glob<{ default: ImageMetadata }>(
  '@/assets/images/cat-*.jpg',
  { eager: true }
);

function getImage(name: string) {
  const key = Object.keys(images).find((k) => k.includes(`/${name}.`));
  if (!key) {
    throw new Error(
      `РќРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅРѕ С„РѕС‚Рѕ С‚РѕРІР°СЂР°: ${name}. РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЊ, С‡С‚Рѕ С„Р°Р№Р» src/assets/images/${name}.jpg СЃСѓС‰РµСЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚.`
    );
  }
  return images[key].default;
}
---

import Container from '@/components/Container.astro';
import { taglines } from '@/content/taglines';

const total = taglines.length;
// Р”Р»РёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ РѕРґРЅРѕРіРѕ С†РёРєР»Р°: 10s РїР°СѓР·Р° + 1.2s РїСЂРѕРєСЂСѓС‚ РЅР° РєР°Р¶РґСѓСЋ С„СЂР°Р·Сѓ
const pause = 10;       // СЃРµРєСѓРЅРґ СЃС‚РѕРёС‚
const roll = 1.2;       // СЃРµРєСѓРЅРґ РїСЂРѕРєСЂСѓС‡РёРІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ
const cycle = total * (pause + roll);
---

<section class="tagline" aria-label="РњР°РЅРёС„РµСЃС‚ РјР°РіР°Р·РёРЅР°">
  <Container>
    <div
      class="tagline__window"
      style={`--total: ${total}; --cycle: ${cycle}s;`}
    >
      <div class="tagline__reel">
        {taglines.map((t) => (
          <span class="tagline__item">{t.text}</span>
        ))}
        {/* Р”СѓР±Р»РёСЂСѓРµРј РїРµСЂРІСѓСЋ вЂ” РґР»СЏ Р±РµСЃС€РѕРІРЅРѕРіРѕ С†РёРєР»Р° */}
        <span class="tagline__item">{taglines[0].text}</span>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="tagline__dots" aria-hidden="true">
      {taglines.map((_, i) => (
        <span
          class="tagline__dot"
          style={`--i: ${i}; --total: ${total}; --cycle: ${cycle}s;`}
        />
      ))}
    </div>
  </Container>
</section>

import '@/styles/tokens.css';
import '@/styles/base.css';
import '@/styles/layout.css';

import Header from '@/components/Header.astro';
import Footer from '@/components/Footer.astro';

import {
  buildTitle,
  buildDescription,
  buildOgImage,
  absoluteUrl,
  withBase,
  localBusinessJsonLd,
} from '@/utils/seo';

interface Props {
  title?: string;
  description?: string;
  image?: string;
  noindex?: boolean;
}

const { title, description, image, noindex = false } = Astro.props;

const pageTitle = buildTitle(title);
const pageDescription = buildDescription(description);
const ogImage = buildOgImage(image);

// canonical вЂ” СЃ СѓС‡С‘С‚РѕРј base. Astro.url.pathname СѓР¶Рµ СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёС‚ base.
const pathname = Astro.url.pathname.replace(/\/$/, '') || '/';
const canonical = absoluteUrl(pathname);

const jsonLd = localBusinessJsonLd();
---

<!doctype html>
<html lang="ru">
  <head>
    <meta charset="utf-8" />
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
    <meta name="generator" content={Astro.generator} />

    <title>{pageTitle}</title>
    <meta name="description" content={pageDescription} />
    <link rel="canonical" href={canonical} />

    {noindex && <meta name="robots" content="noindex,nofollow" />}

    <!-- Open Graph -->
    <meta property="og:type" content="website" />
    <meta property="og:title" content={pageTitle} />
    <meta property="og:description" content={pageDescription} />
    <meta property="og:image" content={ogImage} />
    <meta property="og:url" content={canonical} />
    <meta property="og:locale" content="ru_RU" />
    <meta property="og:site_name" content="Р§СѓР»Р°РЅС‡РёРє" />

    <!-- Twitter -->
    <meta name="twitter:card" content="summary_large_image" />
    <meta name="twitter:title" content={pageTitle} />
    <meta name="twitter:description" content={pageDescription} />
    <meta name="twitter:image" content={ogImage} />

    <!-- Favicon -->
    <link rel="icon" type="image/svg+xml" href={withBase('/favicon.svg')} />

    <!-- Fonts (Google, РїРѕРєР° РЅРµС‚ self-hosted) -->
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" />
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin />
    <link
      href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700&family=Lobster&display=swap"
      rel="stylesheet"
    />

    <!-- JSON-LD -->
    <script type="application/ld+json" set:html={JSON.stringify(jsonLd)} />
  </head>
  <body>
    <Header />
    <main>
      <slot />
    </main>
    <Footer />
  </body>
</html>

==========================================
FILE: src\utils\seo.ts

import { site } from '@/content/site';

// =========================================================
// base РёР· astro.config.mjs РїРѕРїР°РґР°РµС‚ РІ import.meta.env.BASE_URL
// РІ РІРёРґРµ '/chulanchik-cite/' (СЃРѕ СЃР»СЌС€РµРј РЅР° РєРѕРЅС†Рµ). РќРѕСЂРјР°Р»РёР·СѓРµРј.
// =========================================================
const RAW_BASE = import.meta.env.BASE_URL ?? '/';
const BASE = RAW_BASE.endsWith('/') ? RAW_BASE.slice(0, -1) : RAW_BASE;

/**
 * РџСЂРёРєР»РµРёРІР°РµС‚ base Рє Р°Р±СЃРѕР»СЋС‚РЅРѕРјСѓ РїСѓС‚Рё.
 *   withBase('/about')              в†’ '/chulanchik-cite/about'
 *   withBase('/')                   в†’ '/chulanchik-cite/'
 *   withBase('#anchor')             в†’ '#anchor'         (РЅРµ С‚СЂРѕРіР°РµРј)
 *   withBase('https://example.com') в†’ 'https://example.com' (РЅРµ С‚СЂРѕРіР°РµРј)
 *   withBase('mailto:hi@x.ru')      в†’ 'mailto:hi@x.ru'  (РЅРµ С‚СЂРѕРіР°РµРј)
 */
export function withBase(path: string): string {
  if (!path) return BASE || '/';
  if (/^[a-z]+:/i.test(path)) return path;
  if (path.startsWith('#')) return path;
  if (!path.startsWith('/')) return path;
  if (path === '/') return BASE ? `${BASE}/` : '/';
  return `${BASE}${path}`;
}

export interface SeoOptions {
  title?: string;
  description?: string;
  image?: string;
  type?: 'website' | 'article';
  noindex?: boolean;
  canonical?: string;
}

const DEFAULT_IMAGE = '/og-image.png';

export function buildTitle(pageTitle?: string): string {
  if (!pageTitle) return `${site.name} вЂ” ${site.tagline}`;
  return `${pageTitle} вЂ” ${site.name}`;
}

export function buildDescription(desc?: string): string {
  return desc ?? site.description;
}

export function absoluteUrl(path: string): string {
  if (/^https?:/i.test(path)) return path;
  const base = site.url.replace(/\/$/, '');
  const clean = path.startsWith('/') ? path : `/${path}`;
  return `${base}${clean}`;
}

export function buildOgImage(image?: string): string {
  return absoluteUrl(withBase(image ?? DEFAULT_IMAGE));
}

export function localBusinessJsonLd() {
  return {
    '@context': 'https://schema.org',
    '@type': 'LocalBusiness',
    name: site.name,
    description: site.description,
    url: site.url,
    telephone: site.phone,
    email: site.email,
    address: {
      '@type': 'PostalAddress',
      streetAddress: site.address,
      addressCountry: 'RU',
    },
    openingHours: site.hours,
  };
}
